Entry 1
U: I need to pick up a rental car in Philly.
A: You would like it till when?
U: Till next Thursday. I need it for evening 5:30
A: When would you like to pick it up?
U: I would like it from tomorrow.
A: There are 7 options. There is a lovely standard civic car at PHL international airport on March 2nd?
U: Is there anything else? I need a compact car for 6 in the evening.
A: There is a lovely compact cooper car at intercity terminal on March 2nd?
U: Can I have another car for sunday this week?
A: There are 5 options. There is a lovely fit compact car at intercity terminal on March 3rd?
U: How much would it cost in all?
A: $96 in all.
U: That would be great.
A: Would you like the car?
U: No,not right now. I would need flexible tickets for a bus to get there.
A: When would you like to leave and where would you be traveling from?
U: I would be leaving Washington later today.
A: Would you like a flexible ticket for a bus at 7:10 am? It would cost $50.
U: That is too early. Is there anything else leaving tomorrow?
A: There are 7 options available. Would you like a flexible ticket for a bus at 7:50 am? It would cost $45.
U: What is the name of the bus station that I would be leaving from and which one would I be arriving at?
A: You would be leaving Union station and heading to intercity terminal.
U: That would be great.
A: You would like to get tickets?
U: No, I don't.
A: Is that anything I can assist you with?
U: I would like to get an economy seat on a round trip flight for the 12th.
A: Would you like to leave on March 3rd or March 2nd or some other day?
U: I want to leave later today.
A: There is 1 suitable flight. There is an outbound flight on united airlines that leaves at 8:40 pm. It would be returning at 4:45 am and costs $195
U: Is the ticket refundable? How many stops are there?
A: There is just 1. I am sorry but the ticket is non-refundable.
U: That flight would work
A: Is there anything else?
U: No. Thank you.
A: Enjoy your day.
